## Best date plans

### 2026-11-21 — Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café + Pataphysics Audiovisual Concert Series

**Dinner, then Pataphysics Audiovisual Concert Series.** Seasonal American prix fixe at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café. It is 2.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Doors 7:30 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden%2C+enter+at+990+Washington+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=303+Saratoga+Avenue&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.delightfactorynyc.com/events/pataphysics022) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe)

### 2026-11-21 — Fall Saturdays — extended exhibition hours + Runner Up

**Fall Saturdays — extended exhibition hours, then a meal.** Seasonal American supper at Runner Up. It is 1.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.

#### [Lightscape: Opening-Weekend Garden Trail](https://www.bbg.org/lightscape)

Time to confirm · outdoors · Off-peak adult $39; member adult $33; fees included · Brooklyn Botanic Garden · Crown Heights · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
Walk a mile-long illuminated route through the garden's trees, water features and historic landscapes, with seasonal drinks and warming stops.
**Timing:** Evening timed-entry experience. November 21 is confirmed as an off-peak date; select an available fifteen-minute entry window. Exact session hours were not verified.
**Practical note:** Enter only at 990 Washington Avenue. The paved trail is accessible; ordinary garden admission does not include Lightscape.
**Why now:** Opening weekend of the November 20–January 3 season; this date is explicitly enabled in the official calendar.
**On-sale watch:** A date-specific Lightscape ticket and entry window are required.
[Official](https://www.bbg.org/lightscape) · [Tickets / booking](https://events.leapevents.com/tickets/lightscape2026/listing/) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://www.theskint.com/ongoing-events/)

#### [Portraits of the Artist](https://www.anthologyfilmarchives.org/film_screenings/calendar?view=list&month=11&year=2026#showing-62309)

Time to confirm · film · Anthology Film Archives · East Village · Manhattan · Score 54.4  
Beginning with Gerard Malanga’s ANDY WARHOL (1964), in which the author of the SCREEN TESTS sits for his own extended portrait, this program finds the directors turning the camera on their fellow filmmakers, or even trading places for complementary films (as in the case of the Friedl vom Gröller and Christiana Perschon films that close the screening). **Why now:** November 27 is explicitly listed for extended evening access.
**On-sale watch:** Select admission that includes the Holiday Train Show and the desired visit time.

## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 21, 2026: weekday deals, Thanksgiving menus, recent openings and dated pop-ups. Holiday exceptions and remaining availability are not guaranteed.

Dining research: 2026-11-21T10:42:06+00:00

No current Michelin award was independently verified in this review; recognition fields remain empty. Eater heatmap inclusion does not establish Michelin recognition, and Garnet does not inherit its predecessor's awards. Michelin's own listings were not checked. Captured public-source evidence and 12 direct follow-up checks informed these decisions.

### Le Gratin: $45 lunch and $30 bar entrees

Deals & happy hours — Le Gratin

Two-course lunch offers a lower-cost introduction to the restaurant. Separately, the bar serves steak, mussels, chicken or steak tartare with fries for $30 during happy hour.

Price: Lunch: two courses $45 or three $55 per person. Happy-hour entrees $30; mini martinis $8 separately.  
Schedule: Lunch Monday–Friday, 11:30am–2:30pm. Happy hour Tuesday–Friday, 5–7pm.  
Conditions: Happy-hour offers are bar-only; walk-ins welcome. The $8 mini martini is separate from the $30 entree. Lunch supplements include steak tartare +$5, skate +$3, cheeseburger +$5 and steak frites +$8. Tax, gratuity and holiday exclusions unstated. Neither offer runs Saturday.  
[Source](https://legratinnyc.com/experiences)

### Le Gratin's November duck-and-foie-gras pithivier

Special menus — Le Gratin

November's Plat du Mois is a duck-and-foie-gras puff-pastry pie with mushroom farce and truffle-Madeira jus.

Price: $72  
Schedule: Dinner throughout November. Regular dinner hours: Monday–Saturday 5–10pm; Sunday 5–9pm.  
Conditions: Dinner-only monthly special, not part of the $30 happy-hour offer. Serving size, tax, gratuity and holiday exceptions unstated. Reservations offered; remaining availability unverified.  
From: 2026-11-01  
Through: 2026-11-30  
[Source](https://legratinnyc.com/experiences)

### Amélie: $26 weekday lunch and daily early-bird options

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A two-course weekday lunch and a separate burger-or-mussels early-bird offer provide useful Village meal options. Happy hour also includes oysters and three-wine flights.

Price: Two-course lunch $26; lunch wine of the day +$10. Early bird $26 for burger and fries or moules frites with beer or a glass of wine. Happy-hour oysters $1.50 each; six oysters with a glass of wine $19; three-wine flight $14 before 6pm, $19 afterward; selected cocktails $10.  
Schedule: Lunch Monday–Friday noon–3:30pm. Happy-hour food daily 4–6pm; drinks from opening until 6pm.  
Conditions: Lunch excludes tax and gratuity; burger +$5, steak frites or Niçoise salad +$8. Happy-hour food runs 4–6pm; drink discounts run from opening until 6pm. Oyster selection varies. Happy-hour minimums, seating restrictions, tax, gratuity and holiday exceptions unstated.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/happy-hour)

### Junoon's $43 or $49 Prestige lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The lunch set includes basmati rice and naan, with vegetarian and meat choices such as eggplant chaat, mushroom xacutti and shrimp moilee.

Price: Two courses $43; three courses $49.  
Schedule: Official location FAQ lists lunch Friday–Sunday, 11:45am–2:30pm.  
Conditions: Menu states a 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Other tax/gratuity treatment unstated. Location FAQ recommends reservations, welcomes children above age 10 and specifies smart casual attire without gym shorts, tank tops or flip-flops. Holiday exceptions unverified.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### Dynamo Room: $29.95 happy-hour trio and $35.95 express lunch

Deals & happy hours — The Dynamo Room

The happy-hour trio combines a mini dirty martini, Caesar salad and beef-fat fries. Express lunch includes rolls, a salad and a choice of pasta or salmon.

Price: Dynamo Trio $29.95. Express Lunch $35.95; steak frites substitution +$10. Happy-hour oysters: three $9.95, six $18.95 or twelve $36.95. Three beef or maitake sliders $19.95.  
Schedule: Happy hour daily 4–6pm. Express lunch Monday–Thursday, 11:30am–2:30pm.  
Conditions: Happy hour only in the bar or on the terrace. Trio includes a mini, not standard-size, martini. Tax, gratuity, reservation requirements and holiday exclusions unstated. No Friday–Sunday express lunch listed.  
[Source](https://www.thedynamoroom.com/menus/)

### Francie's weekday bar happy hour and conditional Monday corkage waiver

Deals & happy hours — Francie

A bar-only alternative to a full dinner offers small martinis and selected wines. Mondays add a corkage waiver tied to purchasing wine from the restaurant.

Price: Teeny 'Tinis $12; selected wine by the glass $13; olive-oil fries $15. Monday corkage waived with purchase of any bottle from the list.  
Schedule: Monday–Thursday 5:30–7pm; Friday 5–6:30pm.  
Conditions: Happy hour is bar-only, Monday–Friday, not Saturday. Monday's corkage waiver requires purchasing a bottle from the restaurant's list. The menu lists general corkage at $90 per bottle, with a two-bottle-per-party limit; waiver-specific limits are unstated. Tax, gratuity and bar reservation requirements unstated.  
[Source](https://www.franciebrooklyn.com/menus/)

### Sushiro's $2 otoro grand-opening promotion

Deals & happy hours — Sushiro Times Square

The chain's first US location advertises a $2 otoro opening special, providing a specific low-price introduction rather than a discount on an entire meal.

Price: $2 otoro promotional item.  
Schedule: Daily 11am–11pm; last orders 10:30pm.  
Conditions: While supplies last. End date, serving quantity, per-person limits, minimum purchase, tax and gratuity unstated. Online waitlist offered; wait times and remaining promotional stock unverified.  
[Source](https://sushiro-usa.com/promotion)

A twelve-seat bar serving a $70 supper for two, with a recent menu built around seasonal vegetables and pork loin. A distinctive dinner after a park or Green-Wood outing. Official service is Tuesday–Saturday, 5–8:45pm; dessert costs extra.
[Official](https://www.winner.nyc/location/runner-up/) · [Map](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Runner+Up%2C+499+11th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215%2C+New+York%2C+NY)

### [WINNER Bakery](https://www.winner.nyc/location/winner-bakery/)

Bakery, sandwiches and coffee · $ · Park Slope · Brooklyn · Score 55.0  
A practical breakfast or sandwich stop around Prospect Park's southwest side, with pastries around $5–$6.50 and an $8.50 egg sandwich. Primarily takeout with limited outdoor seating. The bakery says it opens 365 days a year; this is not a late dinner option.
[Official](https://www.winner.nyc/location/winner-bakery/) · [Map](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=WINNER+Bakery%2C+367+7th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215%2C+New+York%2C+NY)

### [Altbau Bakery](https://altbaubakery.com/)

Artisan bread, pastries and coffee · $$ · Williamsburg · Brooklyn · Score 50.0  
Chad Robertson's bakery pairs grain-focused breads with Wei Si Phua's pastries. Grub Street identified the October 29 opening, followed by an actual November review. Official hours are Wednesday–Sunday from 9am until sellout; consider it before a Williamsburg outing, not after dinner.
[Official](https://altbaubakery.com/) · [Map](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Altbau+Bakery%2C+184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211%2C+New+York%2C+NY) · [Source: Grub Street](https://www.grubstreet.com/article/chad-robertson-altbau-opening-first-look.html) · [Discovered via The Infatuation NYC](https://www.theinfatuation.com/new-york/reviews/altbau-bakery)

### [Café Fiorello](https://www.cafefiorello.com/)

Italian · Lincoln Square · Manhattan · Score 50.0  
An established Lincoln Center pairing with an antipasto bar and pizza. Official hours support later dining: until 10pm Sunday–Monday, 11pm Tuesday–Thursday and 11:30pm Friday–Saturday.
[Official](https://www.cafefiorello.com/) · [Map](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Caf%C3%A9+Fiorello%2C+1900+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023%2C+New+York%2C+NY) · [Source: Café Fiorello](https://www.cafefiorello.com/location/cafe-fiorello/)

### [Dravida](https://dravidanyc.com/)

Indian and South Asian diaspora · East Village · Manhattan · Score 50.0  
Chef Aarthi Sampath's intimate restaurant explores Indian and wider diaspora flavors. Dinner runs past 9pm Tuesday–Sunday; Monday is closed. Reservations open up to fourteen days ahead. Eater's Thanksgiving lead was not promoted to an event because official holiday-menu details were not verified.
[Official](https://dravidanyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/dravida) · [Map](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Dravida%2C+211+First+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003%2C+New+York%2C+NY) · [Discovered via Eater New York](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-thanksgiving-restaurants-nyc)

American comfort food · Harlem · Manhattan · Score 50.0  
A confirmed Thanksgiving option with both à la carte dining and a $90 turkey prix fixe. The main dining room adds a $15 entertainment cover, with tax and tip extra. November 26 service runs 11am–10pm; expect a lively room rather than a quiet dinner.

French bistro · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 47.0  
A French neighborhood bistro with red booths, a marble bar and natural wines, operating since 2013. Its explicitly published 5–10pm kitchen service makes it a useful dinner after an earlier UrbanGlass appointment or Downtown Brooklyn outing.

Seasonal New American · Bronx Park · Bronx · Score 47.0  
A seated farm-to-table restaurant using Hudson Valley and regional ingredients inside NYBG. Friday service ends at 3pm, so it can precede a longer garden visit but cannot serve as dinner after the retained evening Train Show. Confirm garden admission requirements when reserving.

Persian · Prospect Heights · Brooklyn · Score 46.0

Vegan Ethiopian · Bushwick · Brooklyn · Score 40.0  
Share injera and plant-based Ethiopian dishes before or after the retained 5pm coffee ceremony. First come, first served, with no reservations. Official dinner hours extend past 9pm; the brick-and-mortar restaurant opened in 2014.

#### [Elektra: newly extended December performances](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-11-21-200-pm)

2026-12-08 · 7:30 PM · theater · Regular tickets $70–$98, plus $7 handling · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 68.0  
Daniel Fish directs Sophokles in Anne Carson's translation, with choral music by Ted Hearne. The theater has added December 8–13 performances to the engagement.
**Timing:** December 8 begins the newly extended block, which continues through December 13. Running time approximately 80 minutes without intermission.
**Practical note:** No late seating. Regular tickets are $70–$98 plus a $7 handling fee; separate eligibility-based discounts are available.
**Why now:** The extension offers another booking opportunity amid explicitly documented scarcity in the original run.
**Hot booking alert:** TFANA labels December 8–13 'Newly Extended!' while December 1–3 and the December 6 matinee are sold out and several other original performances have limited to no availability. December 13 is the announced closing date.
